Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in polymers theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 883 and the invariant I(A,B) = 436 mod 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by polymers generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 436. QED.
